#### Softening -- Palatalization

Czech adjectives in *Kdo? Co? TEN animate plural* soften the last
consonant. The rules for softening the consonant are similar to nouns in
the same case.

  -------- ------------------ ----------------
  k **\>** sladký **\>**      (sweet)
  c        sladcí             

  ch       hluchý **\>**      (deaf)
  **\>** š hluší              

  r **\>** starý **\>** staří (old)
  ř                           

  h **\>** *drahý **\>**      (expensive)
  z        drazí*             

  d **\>** mladý **\>** mladí (young)
  ď                           

  n **\>** unavený **\>**     (tired)
  ň        unavení            

  t **\>** *zlatý **\>**      (golden)
  ť        zlatí*             
  -------- ------------------ ----------------

#### Comparisons or More - Most

##### Summary

  ---------------------------------------------------------
  good        better         the best              
  ----------- -------------- --------------------- --------
  dobr**ý**   lep**ší**      **nej**lep**ší**      good

  mal**ý**    men**ší**      **nej**men**ší**      little

  levn**ý**   levn**ější**   **nej**levn**ější**   cheap
  ---------------------------------------------------------

+---------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| dobr**ý**                | jako \...      |                | good like \... |
+--------------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| lep**ší**                | než \...       |                | better than    |
|                          |                |                | \...           |
+--------------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| nejlepší                 | z koho, čeho   |                | the best of    |
|                          | \...           |                | \...           |
+---------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| !       | ADJECTIVE + **noun \>** *Tomáš je* **lepší** student *než já.*\   |
|         | (Tomáš is a better student than I)                                |
|         |                                                                   |
|         | ADVERB + **verb \>** Učí se **lépe** *než já.*\                   |
|         | (S/he learns better than I. \[S/he is a better student than I\])  |
+---------+-------------------------------------------------------------------+

##### Forming the endings

  ------- ----- ----------- ------------- ---------------- ---------- -------------
  -ejší         levný       levnější      nejlevnější                 cheap

  -ější         nový        novější       nejnovější                  new

                zajímavý    zajímavější   nejzajímavější              interesting

                moderní     modernější    nejmodernější               modern

                                                                      

  -ší           mladý       mladší        nejmladší                   young

                starý       starší        nejstarší                   old

                drahý       dražší        nejdražší (h\>ž)            expensive

                                                                      

  -čí           hezký       hezčí         nejhezčí                    pretty

  k\>č          měkký       měkčí         nejměkčí                    soft
  ------- ----- ----------- ------------- ---------------- ---------- -------------

#### How to Form Adjectives

- Adjectives are words that describe properties of other objects.

- The question word for adjectives is *jaký?* vs. *co?* for nouns.

- Adjectives can be either genuine (*nový dům* = new house) or derived
  (*zeleninová pizza* = vegetable pizza)

##### Adjectives not formed from other parts of speech

Most adjectives describe properties of other objects by themselves, and
are not derived from other words:

> **malý** (small), **velký** (large), **celý** (whole), žlutý (yellow),
> **bílý** (white), **nový** (new), **tvrdý** (hard), **veselý**
> (cheerful), **zdravý** (healthy)...

##### Adjectives derived from nouns

+----------------+-------------------+---------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| Of what        | -ový              | oliva - *olivový* (olej), zelenina - *zeleninový* (salát),      |
| substance/     |                   | salám - *salámová* (pizza)                                      |
| origin:        |                   |                                                                 |
+----------------+-------------------+-----------------------------------------------------------------+
|                | -í                | včela - *včelí* (med), ryba - *rybí* (salát), kuře - *kuřecí*   |
|                |                   | (řízek)                                                         |
+----------------+-------------------+-----------------------------------------------------------------+
|                |                   |                                                                 |
+----------------+-------------------+-----------------------------------------------------------------+
| Mostly from    | -ský              | muž - mužský, hora - horský, Ostrava - ostravský, Jihlava -     |
| names of       |                   | jihlavský                                                       |
| occupations,   |                   |                                                                 |
| and            |                   |                                                                 |
+----------------+-------------------+-----------------------------------------------------------------+
| local names.   | -cký              | vesnice - vesnický, hora - hornický                             |
+----------------+-------------------+---------------------------------------+-------------------------+
|                |                   |                                       |                         |
+----------------+-------------------+---------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| Sundry         | -ní               | les - lesní, jaro - jarní,            | Který?                  |
| meanings:      |                   |                                       |                         |
+----------------+-------------------+---------------------------------------+-------------------------+
|                | -ný               | kámen - kamenný                       | Jaký?                   |
+----------------+-------------------+---------------------------------------+-------------------------+
| !              | *ní/ný* + ***k/c*** **\>** **č -** *roční* (pizza), *ovčí* (sýr)                    |
+----------------+-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

##### Adjectives derived from verbs

+-----+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------+
| -cí | prací (prášek)                   | washing (powder-soap)                |
+-----+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------+
|     | balicí (papír)                   | wrapping (paper)                     |
+-----+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------+
|     | holicí (pěna)                    | shaving (foam)                       |
+-----+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------+
| !   | All the above forms are present participles. They are explained in the  |
|     | verbs section.                                                          |
+-----+-------------------------------------------------------------------------+

##### Compound Adjectives

*modr**o**oký* (blue-eyed), *krátk**o**zraký* (near-sighted),
*čern**o**bílý* (black and white) *\
světl**e**modrý* (light blue)

#### Possessive adjectives

In English, possession is expressed by --'*s*. For instance, *John's
father, Jana's book*. In Czech, endings **-ův** or **-in** are used as
in *Johnův otec* - *Janina kniha*. They take different forms in
different cases. See below. The only reason they are called adjectives
is because they are placed in the same position as adjectives and agree
in case with the following noun. Otherwise, their endings are similar to
those of nouns. They are only really used with nouns that describe
animate objects and proper names. Never with nouns like *stůl, auto,
mléko* (the Koho? Čeho? form is used instead).

  ---------------- --------------- ------------ -------------- --------------
                   **Petr**        **Jana**                    

  **Kdo? Co?**     Petr**ův**      Janin        kamarád        TEN

  N                Petr**ova**     Janina       kamarádka      TA

                   Petr**ovo**     Jan**ino**   auto           TO

                   Petr**ovi**     Janini       kamarádi       TI

                   Petr**ovy**     Janiny       kamarádky      TY

  **Koho? Co?**    Petr**ova**     Janina       kamaráda       TEN

  A                Petr**ovu**     Jan**inu**   kamarádku      TA

                   Petr**ovo**     Jan**ino**   auto           TO

                   Petr**ovy**     Jan**iny**   kamarády       TY

  **Koho? Čeho?**  Petr**ova**     Jan**ina**   kamaráda       TEN

  G                Petr**ovy**     Jan**iny**   kamarádky      TA

                   Petr**ova**     Janina       auta           TO

                   Petro**vých**   Janiných     kamarádů       TY

  **Komu? Čemu?**  Petr**ovu**     Janinu       kamarádovi     TEN

  D                Petr**ově**     Janině       kamarádce      TA

                   Petr**ovu**     Jan**inu**   autu           TO

                   Petrovým        Janiným      kamarádům      TY

  **(o) Kom?       Petr**ovu**     Jan**inu**   kamarádovi     TEN
  Čem?**                                                       

  L                Petr**ově**     Jan**ině**   kamarádce      TA

                   Petr**ovu**     Jan**ině**   autu           TO

                   Petrových       Janiných     kamarádech     TY

  **Kým? Čím?**    Petr**ovým**    Janiným      kamarádem      TEN

  I                Petr**ovou**    Janinou      kamarádkou     TA

                   Petr**ovým**    Janiným      autem          TO

                   Petrovými       Janinými     kamarády       TY
  ---------------- --------------- ------------ -------------- --------------

  ---- ------------------------------------------------------------------
  !    In English, the possessive relationship is sometimes expressed by
       *of*. In that case, in Czech the *Koho? Čeho?* case is used. For
       instance, *kamarád Petra*.

  ---- ------------------------------------------------------------------

### Pronouns

#### Personal Pronouns

Personal pronouns have different forms after prepositions, and at the
beginning of sentences. If they are used independently, an unstressed
form is used. Prepositions, however, require a stressed form. Já, ty, on
have a special form used for when the noun is at the beginning of the
sentence or when it is stressed.

##### Without a preposition

  ---------------- ------- ------- ------ ------ ------ ------ ------ ------
  **Kdo? Co?** N   já      ty      on     ona    ono    my     vy     oni

  **Koho? Co?** A  mě      tě      ho,    ji     ho,    nás    vás    je
                                   jej           jej                  

  **Koho? Čeho?**  mě      tě      ho,    jí     ho,    nás    vás    jich
  G                                jej           jej                  

  **Komu? Čemu?**  mi      ti      mu     jí     mu     nám    vám    jim
  D                                                                   

  **(o) Kom?       mně     tobě    něm    ní     něm    nás    vás    nich
  Čem?** L                                                            

  **Kým? Čím?** I  mnou    tebou   jím    jí     jím    námi   vámi   jimi
  ---------------- ------- ------- ------ ------ ------ ------ ------ ------

##### After a preposition

  --------- ------- ------- -------- ------ ------- ------- ------- -------
  **Kdo?    já      ty      on       ona    ono     my      vy      oni
  Co?** N                                                           

  **Koho?   na mě   na tebe na něho\ na ni  na      na nás  na vás  na ně
  Co?** A                   na něj          něho\                   
                                            na něj                  

  **Koho?   beze mě bez     bez      bez ní bez     bez nás bez vás bez
  Čeho?** G         tebe    něho\           něho\                   nich
                            bez něj         bez něj                 

  **Komu?   ke mně  k tobě  k němu   k ní   k němu  k nám   k vám   k nim
  Čemu?** D                                                         

  **(o)     o mně   o tobě  o něm    o ní   o něm   o nás   o vás   o nich
  Kom?                                                              
  Čem?** L                                                          

  **Kým?    se mnou s tebou s ním    s ní   s ním   s námi  s vámi  s nimi
  Čím?** I                                                          
  --------- ------- ------- -------- ------ ------- ------- ------- -------

##### In the beginning of a sentence (Strong forms)

+---------------+--------+--------+--------+-----------------------------+
|               | **já** | **ty** | **on** |                             |
+---------------+--------+--------+--------+-----------------------------+
| **Koho?       | mně    | tebe   | jeho   | Jeho se zeptám. = I will    |
| Čeho?**       |        |        |        | ask HIM.                    |
+---------------+        +--------+--------+-----------------------------+
| **Komu?       |        | tobě   | jemu   | Tobě to dám. = I will give  |
| Čemu?**       |        |        |        | it to *YOU*.                |
+---------------+        +--------+--------+-----------------------------+
| **Koho? Co?** |        | tebe   | jeho   | Jeho vidím. = I can see     |
|               |        |        |        | HIM.                        |
+---------------+--------+--------+--------+-----------------------------+

The remaining pronouns are identical to the forms of pronouns without
preposition.

#### Possessive Pronouns 

Possessive pronouns express the relationship of possession, such as *my,
your, ..., their friend, friends*. In Czech, each possessive pronoun has
several forms marking both the case and gender of the following noun.
Note, also, that Czech possessive pronouns do not have a stressed form
like the English *mine, yours, ...*.

+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+
|          | **já** | **ty** | **on** | **ona** | **ono** | **my** | **vy** | **oni** | **GENDER**  |
+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+
| **Kdo?   | můj    | tvůj   | jeho   | její    | jeho    | náš    | váš    | jejich  | TEN         |
| Co?** N  |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
|          +--------+--------+        |         |         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | moje   | tvoje  |        |         |         | naše   | vaše   |         | TA          |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         +-------------+
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | TO          |
|          +--------+--------+        |         |         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | moji   | tvoji  |        |         |         | naši   | vaši   |         | TI          |
|          +--------+--------+        |         |         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | moje   | tvoje  |        |         |         | naše   | vaše   |         | TY, TA      |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | plural      |
+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+
| **Koho?  | mého   | tvého  | jeho   | jejího  | jeho    | našeho | vašeho | jejich  | TEN animate |
| Co?** A  |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | můj    | tvůj   |        | její    |         | náš    | váš    |         | TEN         |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | inanimate   |
|          +--------+--------+        |         |         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | moji   | tvoji  |        |         |         | naši   | vaši   |         | TA          |
|          +--------+--------+        |         |         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | moje   | tvoje  |        |         |         | naše   | vaše   |         | TO          |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         +-------------+
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | TI,TY,TA    |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | plural      |
+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+
| **Koho?  | mého   | tvého  | jeho   | jejího  | jeho    | našeho | vašeho | jejich  | TEN         |
| Čeho?**  |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
| G        |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mojí   | tvojí  |        | její    |         | naší   | vaší   |         | TA          |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mého   | tvého  |        | jejího  |         | našeho | vašeho |         | TO          |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mých   | tvých  |        | jejích  |         | našich | vašich |         | TI,TY,TA    |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | plural      |
+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+
| **Komu?  | mému   | tvému  | jeho   | jejímu  | jeho    | našemu | vašemu | jejich  | TEN         |
| Čemu?**  |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
| D        |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mojí   | tvojí  |        | její    |         | naší   | vaší   |         | TA          |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mému   | tvému  |        | jejímu  |         | našemu | vašemu |         | TO          |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mým    | tvým   |        | jejím   |         | našim  | vašim  |         | TI,TY,TA    |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | plural      |
+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+
| **(o)    | mém    | tvém   | jeho   | jejím   | jeho    | našem  | vašem  | jejich  | TEN         |
| Kom?     |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
| Čem?** L |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mojí   | tvojí  |        | její    |         | naší   | vaší   |         | TA          |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mém    | tvém   |        | jejím   |         | našem  | vašem  |         | TO          |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mých   | tvých  |        | jejich  |         | našich | vašich |         | TI,TY,TA    |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | plural      |
+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+
| **Kým?   | mým    | tvým   | jeho   | jejím   | jeho    | naším  | vaším  | jejich  | TEN         |
| Čím?** I |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         |             |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mou    | tvou   |        | její    |         | naší   | vaší   |         | TA          |
|          +--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mým    | tvým   |        | jejím   |         | naším  | vaším  |         | TO          |
+----------+--------+--------+        +---------+         +--------+--------+         +-------------+
|          | mými   | tvými  |        | jejími  |         | našimi | vašimi |         | TI,TY,TA    |
|          |        |        |        |         |         |        |        |         | plural      |
+----------+--------+--------+--------+---------+---------+--------+--------+---------+-------------+

#### SVŮJ a tricky possessive pronoun

This pronoun is a rough equivalent of the English *my own, \..., their
own*. It is only used when the possessor is also the subject of the
sentence. This allows us to differentiate two meanings of *He went to
visit his brother*.: *Šel navštívit **jeho** bratra.* (somebody else\'s)
or *Šel navštívit **svého** bratra.* (his own)

  ---------------------- ------------ -----------------------------
  **Kdo? Co?**           does not     
                         exist        

  **Koho? Co?**          **svého**    kamaráda

                         **svůj**     rohlík

                         **svou,      vodu
                         svoji**      

                         **svoje**    pivo

                         **svoje**    kamarády, rohlíky, vody, piva

  **Koho? Čeho?**        **svého**    kamaráda, rohlíku

                         **své,       vody
                         svojí**      

                         **svého**    piva

                         **svých**    kamarádů, rohlíků, vod, piv

  **Komu? Čemu?**        **svému**    kamarádovi, rohlíku

                         **své,       vodě
                         svojí**      

                         **svému**    pivu

                         **svým**     kamrádům, rohlíkům, vodám,
                                      pivům

  **(o) Kom? Čem?**      **svém**     kamarádovi

                         **své,       vodě
                         svojí**      

                         **svém**     pivu

                         **svých**    kamarádech, rohlíkách,
                                      vodách, pivech

  **Kým? Čím?**          **svým**     kamarádem

                         **svou,      vodou
                         svojí**      

                         **svým**     pivem

                         **svými**    kamarády, rohlíky, vodami,
                                      pivy
  ---------------------- ------------ -----------------------------

  --- -------------------------------------------------------------------
  !   Czechs themselves often make mistakes in using *svůj* correctly. It
      is important, however, to be aware of its existence.

  --- -------------------------------------------------------------------

#### Demonstrative Pronouns

+-----------------+------+------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+------+------+------+------+
|                 | **Kdo?**    | **Koho?**     | **Koho?**     | **Komu?**     | **(o)       | **Kým?**    |
|                 |             |               |               |               | Kom?**      |             |
|                 | **Co?**     | **Co?**       | **Čeho?**     | **Čemu?**     |             | **Čím?**    |
|                 |             |               |               |               | **(o)       |             |
|                 |             |               |               |               | Čem?**      |             |
+-----------------+------+------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+------+------+------+------+
|                 | *Sg* | *Pl* | *Sg*  | *Pl*  | *Sg*  | *Pl*  | *Sg*  | *Pl*  | *Sg* | *Pl* | *Sg* | *Pl* |
+-----------------+------+------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+------+------+------+------+
| **Masculine**   | ten  | ti   | toho  | ty    | toho  | těch  | tomu  | těm   | tom  | těch | tím  | těmi |
|                 |      |      |       |       |       |       |       |       |      |      |      |      |
| **(animate)**   |      |      |       |       |       |       |       |       |      |      |      |      |
+-----------------+------+------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+------+------+------+------+
| **Masculine**   | ten  | ty   | ten   | ty    | toho  | ty    | tomu  | těm   | tom  | těch | tím  | těmi |
|                 |      |      |       |       |       |       |       |       |      |      |      |      |
| **(inanimate)** |      |      |       |       |       |       |       |       |      |      |      |      |
+-----------------+------+------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+------+------+------+------+
| **Feminine**    | ta   | ty   | tu    | ty    | té    | těch  | té    | těm   | té   | těch | tou  | těmi |
+-----------------+------+------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+------+------+------+------+
| **Neuter**      | to   | ta   | to    | ta    | toho  | těch  | tomu  | těm   | tom  | těch | tím  | těmi |
+-----------------+------+------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+------+------+------+------+

***Tento, tenhle*** or ***tamten*** are used when we are pointing at one
object or person among several others. ***Ten, ta, to*** are used if
there is no doubt which one we mean. They often correspond to the
English ***the***.

+---------+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           | **TADY**                                      | **TAM**                                   |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           | **written**     | **spoken**                  | **written**     | **spoken**              |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
| **TEN**                   | tento           | [ten]{.underline}hle        | ten             | tam[ten]{.underline}    |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tady[ten]{.underline}       |                 |                         |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | [ten]{.underline}hleten     |                 | tamhle[ten]{.underline} |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tadyhle[ten]{.underline}    |                 |                         |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tady[ten]{.underline}hleten |                 |                         |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
| **TA**                    | tato            | [ta]{.underline}hle         | ta              | tam[ta]{.underline}     |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tady[ta]{.underline}        |                 |                         |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | [ta]{.underline}hleta       |                 | tamhle[ta]{.underline}  |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tady[ta]{.underline}hleta   |                 |                         |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
| **TO**                    | toto            | [to]{.underline}hle         | to              | tam[to]{.underline}     |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tady[to]{.underline}        |                 |                         |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tohle[to]{.underline}       |                 | tamhle[to]{.underline}  |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           |                 | tadytohle[to]{.underline}   |                 |                         |
+---------------------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
|                           | **this**        | **THIS HERE**               | **that**        | **THAT OVER THERE**     |
+---------+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------------------+-----------------+-------------------------+
| !       | In *tento*, *tenhle* etc.. only the *ten, ta, to* part changes.                                             |
+---------+-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

#### Forms of VŠECHNO (every, all)

The singular form is very rarely used except in the neuter, where it
means *everything*.

+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+
| **Singular**   | **Masculine**          | **Feminine**          | **Neuter**            |
+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+
| **Kdo? Co?**   | ten      | všech**en** | t**a**  | všechn**a** | t**o**  | všechn**o** |
+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+
| **Koho? Co?**  | toho/ten | všech**en** | t**u**  | všechn**u** | t**o**  | všechn**a** |
+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+
| **Komu?        | tomu     | všemu       | t**é**  | vší         | tomu    | všemu       |
| Čemu?**        |          |             |         |             |         |             |
+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+
| **Koho?        | toho     | všeho       | t**é**  | vší         | toho    | všeho       |
| Čeho?**        |          |             |         |             |         |             |
+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+
| **Kom? Čem?**  | tom      | vše**m**    | t**é**  | vš**í**     | tom     | vše**m**    |
+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+
| **Kým? Čím?**  | tím      | vš**ím**    | tou     | vš**í**     | tím     | vš**ím**    |
+----------------+----------+-------------+---------+-------------+---------+-------------+

+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+
| **Plural**     | **Masculine**                 | **Feminine**         | **Neuter**          |
+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+
| **Kdo? Co?**   | t**i**/t**y** | všichn**i**/\ | t**y**   | všechny   | t**a**  | všechna   |
|                |               | vše**chny**   |          |           |         |           |
+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+
| **Koho? Co?**  | t**y**        | všechny       | t**y**   | všechny   | t**a**  | všechna   |
+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+
| **Komu?        | těm           | vše**m**      | těm      | vše**m**  | těm     | vše**m**  |
| Čemu?**        |               |               |          |           |         |           |
+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+
| **Koho?        | těch          | vše**ch**     | těch     | vše**ch** | těch    | vše**ch** |
| Čeho?**        |               |               |          |           |         |           |
+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+
| **Kom? Čem?**  | těch          | vše**ch**     | těch     | vše**ch** | těch    | vše**ch** |
+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+
| **Kým? Čím?**  | těmi          | vše**mi**     | těmi     | vše**mi** | těmi    | vše**mi** |
+----------------+---------------+---------------+----------+-----------+---------+-----------+

#### Interrogative (relative), Indefinite and Negative Pronouns and Adverbs

+:-----+:---------------+:------------------+:---------------+:---------------+:---------------+:---------------+
| **?**                 | **Interrogative** | **ně-**        | **Indefinite** | **ni-**        | **Negative**   |
+-----------------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| co                    | what              | něco           | something      | nic            | nothing        |
+-----------------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| kdo                   | who               | někdo          | somebody       | nikdo          | nobody         |
+-----------------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| kde                   | where             | někde          | somewhere      | nikde          | nowhere        |
+-----------------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| kdy                   | when              | někdy          | sometime       | nikdy          | never          |
+-----------------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| jaký                  | what kind         | nějaký         | some kind of   | žádný          | none           |
+-----------------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| jak                   | how               | nějak          | somehow        | nijak          | in no way      |
+-----------------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| který                 | which             | některý        | some           | žádný          | none           |
+------+----------------+-------------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+----------------+
| !    | - *co, kdo* change in the same manner as the case question words.                                      |
|      |                                                                                                        |
|      | - *jaký*, *který* behave as normal adjectives.                                                         |
|      |                                                                                                        |
|      | - *kdy, kde, jak* are adverbs and not pronouns, they were included because they behave in a similar    |
|      |   manner                                                                                               |
+------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

##### Examples of usage:

  ------------------ ------------------- --------------- -----------------
  Vidím něco.        I see something.    Nevím nic.      I don't know
                                                         anything.

  Někdo jde.         Somebody is coming. Nikdo to neví.  Nobody knows it.

  Někde mám peníze.  I have money        Nikde nemám     I don't have a
                     somewhere.          domov.          home anywhere.

  Někdy si zpívám.   Sometimes I sing.   Nikdy nemám     I never have
                                         čas.            time.

  Je tu nějaký muž.  There is some (a)   Není tu žádný   There is no man
                     man here.           muž.            here.

  Nějak to uděláme.  We will do it       Nijak se to     It is not getting
                     somehow.            nelepší.        better in any
                                                         way.

  Někteří lidé jsou  Some people are                     
  bohatí.            rich.                               
  ------------------ ------------------- --------------- -----------------

##### Other prefixes

There are also other prefixes that can be used with these words. For
example:

*lec-/leda(s)* (just any, many) -- *ledakdo* (just anybody)*, leckde*
(just anywhere)

*kde-* (pretty much any) -- *kdekdo*, *kdeco* (cannot be used with
*kde*) (colloquial)

*málo-* (few) of *málokdo* (few people), *málokdy* (not often).

##### Suffixes

All of these words can also be used with suffixes which modify their
meaning in various ways. *Jaký* and *který* take a regular ending before
this suffix (e.g. *s jakýmsi* -- with some...).

--*koli* (--ever) as in *kdokoli* (whoever), *kdekoli* (wherever),
*jakýkoli* (whichever) etc.

*--si* (some--) used as the archaic equivalent of *ně*-- as in *kdosi*
(someone), *cosi* (something), *kdesi* (somewhere), etc. *Kdysi* is now
used mostly in the meaning of *long time ago*.

*--pak* (quizzical enquiry) as in *kdopak* (who might it be), *copak*,
*kdypak* (very colloquial)

#### Reflexive pronouns

The reflexive pronouns refer back to the subject of the sentence. Their
general meaning is ***myself, yourself, itself, \...., themselves***.
The difference between se/si and sebe/sobě is that between unstressed
and stressed form.

  ------------ -------- --------- ---------- ----------- ---------- ----------
               Kdo? Co? Koho? Co? Koho?      Komu? Čemu? Kom? Čem?  Kým? Čím?
                                  Čeho?                             

  unstressed   \-       se        \-         si          \-         \-

  stressed     \-       sebe      sebe       sobě        o sobě     sebou
  ------------ -------- --------- ---------- ----------- ---------- ----------

+---+------------------------------------------------------------------+
| ! | - Unlike English, however, Czech only uses one form *se* or *si* |
|   |   for all subjects                                               |
|   |                                                                  |
|   | - *se* has the meaning of *Koho? Co?*                            |
|   |                                                                  |
|   | - *si* has the meaning of *Komu? Čemu?*                          |
+---+------------------------------------------------------------------+

##### Uses of *SE*

NO MEANING: verbs where *se* is only a part of the verb

  ------------- --------------- ---------------------
  ptát se       Koho? Čeho?     to ask

  smát se       Komu? Čemu?     to laugh

  bát se        Koho? Čeho?     to be afraid
  ------------- --------------- ---------------------

PASSIVE: *se* adds the meaning of general subject

+--------------+-------------+--------------------+
| Kouří se     |             | Does one smoke     |
| tady?        |             | here?              |
+--------------+-------------+--------------------+
| Jde se?      |             | Shall we go?       |
+--------------+-------------+--------------------+
| To se        |             | It\'s not said.    |
| neříká.      |             |                    |
|              |             | One/you doesn\'t   |
|              |             | say that.          |
+--------------+-------------+--------------------+

- RECIPROCITY: signifies relationship between two or more objects

+----------+------------+-----------------+
| Známe    |            | We know each    |
| se.      |            | other.          |
+----------+------------+-----------------+
| Sejdeme  |            | We will meet    |
| se.      |            | together.       |
+----------+------------+-----------------+
| Milujeme |            | We love each    |
| se.      |            | other.          |
+----------+------------+-----------------+
| Potkáme  |            | We will meet    |
| se.      |            | (run into) one  |
|          |            | another.        |
+----------+------------+-----------------+
| !        | If a verb has *SE*, the      |
|          | following noun cannot be in  |
|          | *Koho? Co?* case.            |
+----------+------------------------------+

##### Uses of *SI*

TO ONESELF

  ------------- ----------- ------------------------
  Dám si.                   I will have.

  Koupím si.                I will buy.
  ------------- ----------- ------------------------

RECIPROCITY

  ----------------------- -- --------------------------------
  Píšeme si.                 We write to each other.

  Dáváme si dary.            We give each other presents.
  ----------------------- -- --------------------------------

CASUAL ATTITUDE

  ----------------------- -- ------------------------------------------
  Vejde si do hospody.       So, he walks into a bar, just like that.

  Píská si vesele.           He is whistling away happily.
  ----------------------- -- ------------------------------------------

  ------- ------------------------------------------------------------------
  **!**   If a verb has *SI*, the following noun cannot be in *Komu? Čemu?*
          case.

  ------- ------------------------------------------------------------------

### Numerals

#### Counting from 1 to 5 billion

  ------ ---------------- ----- --------------- ------- -------------------
  1      jedna            11    jedenáct        21      dvacet jedna

  2      dva              12    dvanáct         22      dvacet dva

  3      tři              13    třináct         23      dvacet tři

  4      čtyři            14    čtrnáct         24      dvacet čtyři

  5      pět              15    patnáct         25      dvacet pět

  6      šest             16    šestnáct        26      dvacet šest

  7      sedm             17    sedmnáct        27      dvacet sedm

  8      osm              18    osmnáct         28      dvacet osm

  9      devět            19    devatenáct      29      dvacet devět

  10     deset            20    dvacet          30      třicet
  ------ ---------------- ----- --------------- ------- -------------------

+----+-------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 10               | deset          | 100         | sto         | 1 000       | tisíc       |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 20               | dvacet         | 200         | dvě stě     | 2 000       | dva tisíce  |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 30               | třicet         | 300         | tři sta     | 5 000       | pět tisíc   |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 40               | čtyřicet       | 400         | čtyři sta   | 100 000     | sto tisíc   |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 50               | padesát        | 500         | pět set     | 1 000 000   | milión      |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 60               | šedesát        | 600         | šest set    | 2 000 000   | dva milióny |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 70               | sedmdesát      | 700         | sedm set    | 5 000 000   | pět miliónů |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+
| 80               | osmdesát       | 800         | osm set     | 1 000 000   | miliarda    |
|                  |                |             |             | 000         |             |
+------------------+----------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+-------------+

|                  |                |             |             | 000         | miliardy    |

| 100              | sto            | 1 000       | tisíc       | 5 000 000   | pět miliard |
|                  |                |             |             | 000         |             |

| !  | ***čtyři*** (4) is pronounced as ***štyry -*** this pronunciation is extended to 14, |
|    | 40, 400, etc.                                                                        |

#### 0 and negative numbers

> 0 nula
>
> -1 mínus jedna

#### Decimals and Fractions

Decimals are written with a comma as in **1,5** which is pronounced
**celá** (whole) as in **jedna celá pět**.

Decimals between 0 and 1 are always pronounced with zero as in 0,2
**nula celá dva**.

Fraction names are formed by adding the ending **--ina** to the number.

> pět pět**ina**
>
> deset deset**ina**

Fractions formed from 2 - 4 have irregular forms

> dva **polovina** (half)
>
> tři tř**etina** (third)
>
> čtyři čt**vrtina** (quarter)

#### Types of numerals

Numerals are much trickier in Czech than English. Here is a summary of
the different types of numerals:

  ------ -------------------- ----------------- ------------------- --------------------- --------------------
         **cardinal**         **ordinal**       **name**            **multiplication**    **generic**

  1      jedna                prvn**í**         jedn**ička**        jednou                jedny

  2      dva                  druh**ý**         dvojka              dvakrát               dvoje

  3      tři                  třet**í**         trojka              třikrát               troje

  4      čtyři                čtvrt**ý**        čtyř**ka**          čtyři**krát**         čtv**ery**

  5      pět                  p**á**t**ý**      pět**ka**           pětkrát               p**a**t**ery**

  6      šest                 šest**ý**         šest**ka**          šest**krát**          šest**ery**

  7      sedm                 sedm**ý**         sedm**ička**        sedm**krát**          sedm**ery**

  8      osm                  osm**ý**          osmička             osmkrát               osm**ery**

  9      devět                dev**á**t**ý**    devít**ka**         devět**krát**         devat**ery**

  10     deset                des**á**t**ý**    desít**ka**         deset**krát**         desat**ery**

  11     jede**náct**         jedenáct**ý**     jednáct**ka**       jedenáct**krát**      jedenáct**ery**

  12     dva**náct**          dvanáct**ý**      dvanáct**ka**       dvanáct**krát**       dvanáct**ery**

  13     tři**náct**          třináct**ý**      třináct**ka**       třináct**krát**       třináct**ery**

  14     čtr**náct**          čtrnáct**ý**      čtrnáct**ka**       čtrnáct**krát**       čtrnáct**ery**

  15     p**a**t**náct**      patnáct**ý**      patnáct**ka**       patnáct**krát**       patnáct**ery**

  16     šest**náct**         šestnáct**ý**     šestnáct**ka**      šestnáct**krát**      šestnáct**ery**

  \...                                                                                    

  19     dev**a**te**náct**   devatenáct**ý**   devatenáct**ka**    devatenáct**krát**    

  20     dva**cet**           dvac**átý**       dvac**ítka**        dvacet**krát**        dvac**a**t**ery**

  21     dvacet jedna         dvac**átý**       jednadvacít**ka**   jednadvacet**krát**   jednadvaca**tery**
                              prvn**í**                                                   

  ...                                                                                     

  50     padesát              padesát**ý**      padesát**ka**       padesát**krát**       padesát**ery**

  ...                                                                                     

  100    sto                  st**ý**           st**ovka**          stokrát               stery

  1000   tisíc                tisíc**í**        tisíc**ovka**       tisíc**krát**         tisícery
  ------ -------------------- ----------------- ------------------- --------------------- --------------------

  --- --------------------------------------------------------------------
  !   Numbers like 21, 22, ..., 99 are often spelled out in the German or
      old English manner as ***jednadvacet, dva a dvacet, ..., devět a
      devadesát***.

  --- --------------------------------------------------------------------

#### Case and numbers

Yes. Regrettably, all the Czech numbers have cases. Therefore, you use a
different form when you say *I\'ll have **two** dumplings* (dám si dva
knedlíky) and *I\'ll have it **with** [two]{.underline} dumplings* (dám
si to se dvěma knedlíky.) Also the endings are different for ***one***,
and ***two, three, four*** and ***five** and more*.

##### Cardinal numbers 1, 2, 3, 4

*Cardinal* numbers change with case according to the chart

+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------+------------------+-----------------+
| jeden, jedna,   | ten                                                 | ta               | to              |
| -o              |                                                     |                  |                 |
+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------------------------+------------------+-----------------+
| **Kdo? Co?**    | jeden           |                                   | jedna            | jedno           |
+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------------------------+------------------+-----------------+
| **Koho? Co?**   | jeden           | jednoho                           | jednu            | jedno           |
+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------------------------+------------------+-----------------+
| **Koho? Čeho?** | jednoho         |                                   | jedné            | jednoho         |
+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------+------------------+-----------------+
| **Komu? Čemu?** | jednomu                           |                 | jedné            | jednomu         |
+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------+------------------+-----------------+
| **(o) Kom?      | jednom          |                                   | jedné            | jednom          |
| Čem?**          |                 |                                   |                  |                 |
+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------------------------+------------------+-----------------+
| **Kým? Čím?**   | jedním          |                                   | jednou           | jedním          |
+-----------------+-----------------+-----------------------------------+------------------+-----------------+
| **!** 1 endings are very similar to those of *ten, ta, to*                                                 |
+------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

  ----------------- ------------------- ------------------- -----------
  dva, tři, čtyři   2                   3                   4

  **Kdo? Co?**      dva - dvě - dvě     tři                 čtyři

  **Koho? Co?**     dva - dvě - dvě     tři                 čtyři

  **Koho? Čeho?**   dvou                tří                 čtyř

  **Komu? Čemu?**   dvěma               třem                čtyřem

  **(o) Kom? Čem?** dvou                třech               čtyřech

  **Kým? Čím?**     dvěma               třema               čtyřma
  ----------------- ------------------- ------------------- -----------

  ---- ------------------------------------------------------------------
  !    *třema, čtyřma* are *třemi, čtyřmi* in literary Czech; *dvěma,*
       however, is the correct form in both literary and colloquial
       Czech. It is often confusing for Czechs, too.

  ---- ------------------------------------------------------------------

##### Cardinal numbers 5 and more

Numbers from **5** through **99** use the ending ***-i*** for all cases
except for *Koho? Co?* which stays the same as *Kdo? Co?*

In compound numbers all the components have the ending. E.g. *s
dvacet**i** pět**i**, bez dvacet**i** **dvou***.

100 (**sto**) has the same endings as *pivo* but doesn\'t change in
daily spoken Czech

1 000 (**tisíc**) has the same endings as *čaj*

1 000 000 (**milión**) has the same endings as *rohlík*

1 000 000 000 (**miliarda**) has the same endings as *žena*

##### Other numbers

*Ordinal* numbers (first, second, etc.) behave as adjectives: (*první,
druhý, třetí, ...*)

The *names* of numbers behave as feminine nouns: (*jednička, dvojka,
trojka,* ...)

*Multiplication* numbers do not change their forms with case: (*jednou,
dvakrát*, *třikrát, ...*)

*Generic* numbers don\'t often have to change their forms: (*jedny,
dvoje, troje, ...*) but if necessary they will adopt the forms of the
plural of feminine nouns ending in *-a*.

##### Number names

Numbers have special forms for their names. These might be used for
instance when referring to numbered trains, players or grades. In
English, just the number or the word Number is used.

The number name is formed using the ending **-ka**. This ending often
modifies the form of the preceding number. See table above for
exceptions.

9, 10, 20, 30, 40 **-ítka** (replaces **-et** as in dev**ět** -
dev**ítka**)

33 second number is pronounced first followed by **a** as in
**tři[a]{.underline}třic[ítka]{.underline}**

450 **-ka** is only added to the last number as in
**čtyřistapadesát[ka]{.underline}**

200 -- 900 the first number uses the Koho? Čeho? form as in 500 =
**pět[i]{.underline}stovka**

##### *all* and *every/each*

+----------------------------------+-----------------------------------+
| **All / Všichni**                | **Every / Každý**                 |
+----------------------------------+-----------------------------------+
| Všichni studenti umějí číst.     | Každý člověk má srdce.            |
|                                  |                                   |
| (All students can read.)         | (Every/Each person has a heart.)  |
+----------------------------------+-----------------------------------+

#### Time telling

There are two systems for telling time in Czech. Everybody understands
the digital or military time which is used with a 24 hour clock but
there is also another, more devious, way of telling time in Czech based
on the 12 hour clock.

This system shows time in five-minute intervals with respect to
fifteen-minute intervals on the dial. That is the top of the hour,
quarter of the hour, bottom of the hour, and three quarters of the hour.
So the following system is used:

  ------ ------ ----------------------------- -- ------------------------------
         5:55   za pět minut šest                six in 5 minutes

  6:00          šest                             six

         6:05   šest a pět minut                 six and 5 minutes

                                                 

         6:10   *za pět minut* čtvrt na sedm     a quarter of the way to 7 in 5
                                                 minutes

  6:15          čtvrt na sedm                    a quarter of the way to 7

         6:20   čtvrt na sedm *a pět minut*      a quarter of the way to 7 and
                                                 5 minutes

                                                 

         6:25   za pět minut půl sedmé           half of the 7**th** in 5
                                                 minutes

  6:30          půl sedmé                        half of the 7**th**

         6:35   půl sedmé a pět minut            half of the 7**th** and 5
                                                 minutes

                                                 

         6:40   *za pět minut* tři čtvrtě na     three quarters of the way to 7
                sedm                             in 5 minutes

  6:45          tři čtvrtě na sedm               three quarters of the way to 7

         6:50   tři čtvrtě na sedm *a pět        three quarters of the way to 7
                minut*                           and 5 minutes
  ------ ------ ----------------------------- -- ------------------------------

- with *half*, the number of the hour is given in the ordinal form
  (2nd-12th) in the *Koho? Čeho?* case form (the exception is 1); the
  other three points of reference use *na* + the normal number in *Koho?
  Co?* (thus *jednu*)

- this system is used with the 12 hour dial **only -** *~~za pět minut
  šestnáct~~*

- it is also possible to use 10-minute increments; thus: 5:50 - za deset
  minut šest, 6:10 - šest a deset minut, 6:20 - za deset minut půl
  sedmé, 6:35 - za deset minut tři čtvrtě na sedm

+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
|     | 00       |   | 15                |   | 30                 |   | 45                 |
+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
| 1:  | jedna    |   | čtvrt  | dvě      |   | půl    | druhé     |   | tři     | dvě      |
|     |          |   | na     |          |   |        |           |   | čtvrtě  |          |
|     |          |   |        |          |   |        |           |   | na      |          |
+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
| 2:  | dvě      |   | čtvrt  | tři      |   | půl    | třetí     |   | tři     | tři      |
|     |          |   | na     |          |   |        |           |   | čtvrtě  |          |
|     |          |   |        |          |   |        |           |   | na      |          |
+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
| 3:  | tři      |   | čtvrt  | čtyři    |   | půl    | čtvrté    |   | tři     | čtyři    |
|     |          |   | na     |          |   |        |           |   | čtvrtě  |          |
|     |          |   |        |          |   |        |           |   | na      |          |
+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
| 4:  | čtyři    |   | čtvrt  | pět      |   | půl    | páté      |   | tři     | pět      |
|     |          |   | na     |          |   |        |           |   | čtvrtě  |          |
|     |          |   |        |          |   |        |           |   | na      |          |
+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
| 5:  | pět      |   | čtvrt  | šest     |   | půl    | šesté     |   | tři     | šest     |
|     |          |   | na     |          |   |        |           |   | čtvrtě  |          |
|     |          |   |        |          |   |        |           |   | na      |          |
+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
| 6:  | šest     |   | čtvrt  | sedm     |   | půl    | sedmé     |   | tři     | sedm     |
|     |          |   | na     |          |   |        |           |   | čtvrtě  |          |
|     |          |   |        |          |   |        |           |   | na      |          |
+-----+----------+---+--------+----------+---+--------+-----------+---+---------+----------+
